In 2002, in our country, the production of crustaceans per decarnated nuts dropped to 113 kilograms, while in 2015, it dropped to 78 kilograms. Efficiency decline continues, and this is an important problem. The error in production and price policy is the fundamental problems of nuts. The monopoly situation, controlled by one or several companies, today has reduced the price of nuts to much less than its cost, up to $8. The failure of the nut to reach the values it deserves also nullifies the manufacturer's expectation of prosperity growth. The manufacturer's share of national income is relative to diminishing. According to the general socioeconomic development index of the provinces, the army, which produced nuts, Giresun and Samsun, have fallen behind in its level of development. According to the 2000-2011 comparison, Giresun was ranked number 52, 57, and Samsun was ranked ranked ranked ranked number 61, and 27. These indicators show that our economies, based on nuts, are falling behind, and the manufacturers, whose livelihood is nuts, are becoming poor. The other important problem with the nut manufacturer is that it is concerned that due to the height of input costs, it will not be able to cover the cost of production. The most important element of nut production costs is fertilizer, medicine, fuel and labor. Increased costs of fertilizer, medicine, diesel and maintenance are negative for production. Although nut manufacturers may seem to be supported, these support policies do not eliminate the small manufacturer's dependence on the merchant or the loan shark. The major problems facing the nut maker today are these: the lack of support, guarantee price and production planning policies within scientific context; the inability to implement the disease in nut trees and the negativity created by the inferior product; the lack of efficiency related to frost and other natural disasters; the lack of promotion and market research policies that can export all the produce; the lack of nut added value to a high export product; the inability of the local person to migrate to major cities; the lack of natural problems, the low efficiency of the FISKOBIRYLEM, due to the lack of freezing and other natural disasters; and the lack of supplying of the production of products, and the lack of the production of the production of the products, and the lack of the production of these are the number of the number of producers, and the number of producers. With a guarantee price application on nuts and some other strategic products, the price will be determined together, not by alivrecists, but by producers, agricultural chambers and other relevant organisations.
